NotificationWorker\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/worker/NotificationWorker.kt`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create the worker**\n\n```kotlin\npackage pub.hackers.android.data.worker\n\nimport android.Manifest\nimport android.app.NotificationManager\nimport android.app.PendingIntent\nimport android.content.Context\nimport android.content.Intent\nimport android.content.pm.PackageManager\nimport android.os.Build\nimport androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat\nimport androidx.core.content.ContextCompat\nimport androidx.hilt.work.HiltWorker\nimport androidx.work.CoroutineWorker\nimport androidx.work.WorkerParameters\nimport dagger.assisted.Assisted\nimport dagger.assisted.AssistedInject\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first\nimport pub.hackers.android.MainActivity\nimport pub.hackers.android.R\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository\nimport pub.hackers.android.domain.model.Notification\n\n@HiltWorker\nclass NotificationWorker @AssistedInject constructor(\n    @Assisted appContext: Context,\n    @Assisted workerParams: WorkerParameters,\n    private val repository: HackersPubRepository,\n    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,\n    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager\n) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, workerParams) {\n\n    companion object {\n        const val CHANNEL_ID = \"hackerspub_notifications\"\n        const val NOTIFICATION_GROUP = \"pub.hackers.android.NOTIFICATIONS\"\n        const val SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID = 0\n    }\n\n    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {\n        val isLoggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()\n        if (!isLoggedIn) return Result.success()\n\n        val result = repository.getNotifications(refresh = true)\n\n        return result.fold(\n            onSuccess = { notificationsResult ->\n                val notifications = notificationsResult.notifications\n                if (notifications.isEmpty()) return@fold Result.success()\n\n                val newestId = notifications.first().id\n                notificationStateManager.updateLastPolledId(newestId)\n\n                if (hasNotificationPermission()) {\n                    showNotifications(notifications)\n                }\n\n                Result.success()\n            },\n            onFailure = { error ->\n                // Auth errors — don't retry\n                val message = error.message ?: \"\"\n                if (message.contains(\"401\") || message.contains(\"403\") || message.contains(\"unauthorized\", ignoreCase = true)) {\n                    return@fold Result.success()\n                }\n                // Network errors — retry with exponential backoff\n                Result.retry()\n            }\n        )\n    }\n\n    private fun hasNotificationPermission(): Boolean {\n        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {\n            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(\n                applicationContext,\n                Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS\n            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED\n        } else {\n            true\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun showNotifications(notifications: List<Notification>) {\n        val notificationManager = applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager\n\n        val intent = Intent(applicationContext, MainActivity::class.java).apply {\n            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP\n            putExtra(\"navigate_to\", \"notifications\")\n        }\n        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(\n            applicationContext,\n            0,\n            intent,\n            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE\n        )\n\n        // Show individual notifications (up to 5)\n        notifications.take(5).forEachIndexed { index, notification ->\n            val text = buildNotificationText(notification)\n            val builder = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, CHANNEL_ID)\n                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)\n                .setContentTitle(\"Hackers' Pub\")\n                .setContentText(text)\n                .setAutoCancel(true)\n                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)\n                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)\n\n            notificationManager.notify(index + 1, builder.build())\n        }\n\n        // Summary notification for grouping\n        if (notifications.size > 1) {\n            val summary = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, CHANNEL_ID)\n                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)\n                .setContentTitle(\"Hackers' Pub\")\n                .setContentText(\"${notifications.size} new notifications\")\n                .setAutoCancel(true)\n                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)\n                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)\n                .setGroupSummary(true)\n                .build()\n            notificationManager.notify(SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID, summary)\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun buildNotificationText(notification: Notification): String {\n        val actorName = notification.actors.firstOrNull()?.name ?: \"Someone\"\n        return when (notification) {\n            is Notification.Follow -> \"$actorName followed you\"\n            is Notification.Mention -> \"$actorName mentioned you\"\n            is Notification.Reply -> \"$actorName replied to your post\"\n            is Notification.Quote -> \"$actorName quoted your post\"\n            is Notification.Share -> \"$actorName shared your post\"\n            is Notification.React -> \"$actorName reacted to your post\"\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify it compiles**\n\nRun: `cd /Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android && ./gradlew compileReleaseKotlin 2>&1 | tail -5`\n\nExpected: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/worker/NotificationWorker.kt\ngit commit -m \"feat: add NotificationWorker for background notification polling\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: Wire up AppViewModel with unread state and work scheduling\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/AppViewModel.kt:1-16`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Rewrite AppViewModel**\n\nReplace the entire file:\n\n```kotlin\npackage pub.hackers.android.ui\n\nimport androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel\nimport androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope\nimport androidx.work.Constraints\nimport androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy\nimport androidx.work.NetworkType\nimport androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder\nimport androidx.work.WorkManager\nimport dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.launch\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.PreferencesManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.worker.NotificationWorker\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit\nimport javax.inject.Inject\n\n@HiltViewModel\nclass AppViewModel @Inject constructor(\n    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,\n    val preferencesManager: PreferencesManager,\n    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager,\n    private val workManager: WorkManager\n) : ViewModel() {\n\n    companion object {\n        const val NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME = \"notification_poll\"\n    }\n\n    val isLoggedIn: Flow<Boolean> = sessionManager.isLoggedIn\n\n    val hasUnread: StateFlow<Boolean> = notificationStateManager.hasUnread\n        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000), false)\n\n    init {\n        ensureNotificationPolling()\n    }\n\n    private fun ensureNotificationPolling() {\n        viewModelScope.launch {\n            val loggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()\n            if (loggedIn) {\n                enqueueNotificationPolling()\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fun enqueueNotificationPolling() {\n        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()\n            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)\n            .build()\n\n        val workRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<NotificationWorker>(\n            15, TimeUnit.MINUTES\n        ).setConstraints(constraints).build()\n\n        workManager.enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(\n            NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME,\n            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP,\n            workRequest\n        )\n    }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify it compiles**\n\nRun: `cd /Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android && ./gradlew compileReleaseKotlin 2>&1 | tail -5`\n\nExpected: `BUILD androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts\nimport androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize\nimport androidx.compose.material3.Surface\nimport androidx.compose.runtime.getValue\nimport androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf\nimport androidx.compose.runtime.setValue\nimport androidx.compose.ui.Modifier\nimport androidx.core.content.ContextCompat\nimport dagger.hilt.android.AndroidEntryPoint\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.ui.HackersPubApp\nimport pub.hackers.android.ui.theme.HackersPubTheme\nimport pub.hackers.android.ui.theme.LocalAppColors\nimport javax.inject.Inject\n\ndata class DeepLinkData(\n    val token: String,\n    val code: String\n)\n\n@AndroidEntryPoint\nclass MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {\n\n    @Inject\n    lateinit var sessionManager: SessionManager\n\n    private var deepLinkData by mutableStateOf<DeepLinkData?>(null)\n\n    private val requestPermissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(\n        ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()\n    ) { _: Boolean ->\n        // Permission result — no action needed, worker checks at post time\n    }\n\n    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {\n        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)\n        handleDeepLink(intent)\n        requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded()\n        enableEdgeToEdge()\n        setContent {\n            HackersPubTheme {\n                Surface(\n                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),\n                    color = LocalAppColors.current.background\n                ) {\n                    HackersPubApp(deepLinkData = deepLinkData)\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    override fun onNewIntent(intent: Intent) {\n        super.onNewIntent(intent)\n        handleDeepLink(intent)\n    }\n\n    private fun requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded() {\n        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {\n            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(\n                    this, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS\n                ) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED\n            ) {\n                requestPermissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun handleDeepLink(intent: Intent?) {\n        val data = intent?.data ?: return\n        if (data.scheme == \"hackerspub\" && data.host == \"verify\") {\n            val token = data.getQueryParameter(\"token\")\n            val code = data.getQueryParameter(\"code\")\n            if (token != null && code != null) {\n                deepLinkData = DeepLinkData(token = token, code = code)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify it ---

## ISSUES_FOUND

Overall the plan is well-structured, logically ordered, and covers the spec's requirements comprehensively. The code quality is high and the Kotlin/Android patterns are correct. However, I found several issues of varying severity that should be addressed before implementation begins.

---

### Critical Issues

**C1. Line number references in Task 1 Step 2 are wrong**

The plan says to add library entries "after line 45 (`kotlinx-coroutines-play-services`)". In the actual file `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/gradle/libs.versions.toml`, `kotlinx-coroutines-play-services` is on line **45**, but line 46 onward already contains the `mlkit-translate` and `mlkit-language-id` entries (lines 47-48) followed by a blank line and `[plugins]` at line 50. The new entries should be added after line 48 (after the mlkit entries, before the blank line preceding `[plugins]`), or after line 45 as stated but the implementer should be aware of the mlkit entries that now exist between `kotlinx-coroutines-play-services` and `[plugins]`. The instruction text says "after line 45" which is technically correct for placement, but the actual insertion would land between `kotlinx-coroutines-play-services` and `mlkit-translate`, splitting the mlkit block. It would be cleaner to insert after line 48 (after `mlkit-language-id`).

**C2. Line number references in Task 1 Step 1 are wrong**

The plan says to add version entries "after line 15 (`coroutines = "1.9.0"`)" but in the actual file, line 15 is `coroutines = "1.9.0"` followed by `mlkitTranslate = "17.0.3"` on line 16 and `mlkitLanguageId = "17.0.6"` on line 17. Inserting after line 15 would place the new versions between `coroutines` and `mlkitTranslate`, which works but disrupts the existing grouping. Should be "after line 17" to insert after all existing version entries.

**C3. Line number reference in Task 1 Step 3 is wrong**

The plan says to add dependencies "after line 94 (`implementation(libs.hilt.navigation.compose)`)". In the actual file, `implementation(libs.hilt.navigation.compose)` is on line **94**, but lines 96-105 contain apollo, coil, coroutines, and mlkit dependencies. Adding WorkManager dependencies right after line 94 would place them in the middle of the dependencies block between Hilt and Apollo. A more logical placement would be after line 105 (after the mlkit entries) or after line 94 as a grouped Hilt+Work block. Either way, the line number is technically usable but the plan should be explicit that lines 96+ already exist.

**C4. NotificationWorker accesses `notificationsResult.notifications` without checking the comparison against last-polled ID**

Per the spec (Section "Execution flow", point 4): "Compare first notification ID against last polled ID. If different: update..." The plan's `NotificationWorker.doWork()` always calls `updateLastPolledId` and `showNotifications` without comparing the newest ID against the previously polled ID. This means every 15-minute poll will post duplicate system notifications even when there are no new notifications. The worker should read the current `LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID` from DataStore and only update + show notifications when the newest ID differs.

File: Plan line 363-379 (NotificationWorker `doWork()`)

**Recommended fix:** Add a method to `NotificationStateManager` to read the current last-polled ID, then compare in the worker:

```kotlin
// In NotificationStateManager:
suspend fun getLastPolledId(): String? = dataStore.data.map { it[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID] }.first()

// In doWork():
val newestId = notifications.first().id
val previousId = notificationStateManager.getLastPolledId()
if (newestId != previousId) {
    notificationStateManager.updateLastPolledId(newestId)
    if (hasNotificationPermission()) {
        showNotifications(notifications)
    }
}
```

---

### Important Issues

**I1. Spec says "Prompt after first successful login, not on cold start" -- plan does the opposite**

The spec at line 135 states: "Prompt after first successful login, not on cold start." But the plan's Task 11 adds `requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded()` in `MainActivity.onCreate()`, which fires on every cold start regardless of login state. The plan also injects `SessionManager` into `MainActivity` (line 807-808) but never uses it for gating the permission request. The spec is violated here.

To fix, either: (a) check `sessionManager.isLoggedIn` before requesting the permission, or (b) move the permission request to after the sign-in flow completes (e.g., in the `SignInScreen`'s `onSignInSuccess` callback or in `AppViewModel` after detecting login state change). The plan's current `MainActivity` even imports `SessionManager` and `@Inject`s it but never references it -- dead code.

**I2. Plan does not wire notification polling after login**

The spec says (line 98-99): "After login: Enqueue `PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder`..." The plan's `AppViewModel.init` handles the "already logged in on app launch" case, but there is no step to enqueue polling after a fresh login. `SignInScreen`/`SignInViewModel` calls `onSignInSuccess` which navigates to the Timeline, but `AppViewModel` is already initialized at that point with `loggedIn = false` and the `init` block will not re-run. The polling work will not be enqueued until the next app cold start.

**Recommended fix:** Either (a) add a public method in `AppViewModel` that `HackersPubApp` calls reactively when `isLoggedIn` transitions to `true`, or (b) call `appViewModel.enqueueNotificationPolling()` from the composable when `isLoggedIn` changes. For example:

```kotlin
// In HackersPubApp:
LaunchedEffect(isLoggedIn) {
    if (isLoggedIn) {
        viewModel.enqueueNotificationPolling()
    }
}
```

**I3. SettingsViewModel line numbers for constructor are off**

The plan (Task 10, Step 1) says "Change line 37-42 from:" and shows a constructor starting with `class SettingsViewModel @Inject constructor(`. The actual file has:
- Line 36: `@HiltViewModel` 
- Lines 37-43: the constructor

The line range `37-42` in the plan misses line 43 (`) : ViewModel() {`). The "from" snippet in the plan does include the closing `) : ViewModel() {`, so the content is correct but the line range should be `37-43`.

**I4. SettingsViewModel signOut line numbers are off**

The plan (Task 10, header) says "Modify: lines 127-137" for the signOut method. The actual `signOut()` is on lines **127-137** which matches. However, the plan's Step 3 "from" snippet shows the method content, and it does match. This is correct -- no issue here on closer inspection.

**I5. Missing `Context` import in SettingsViewModel Step 2**

The plan adds these imports to `SettingsViewModel`:
```kotlin
import android.app.NotificationManager
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager
import pub.hackers.android.ui.AppViewModel
```

But the `signOut()` method already references `context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE)` using `Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE`. The existing file already imports `android.content.Context` (line 3), so this is fine. However, the plan should note that the existing `Context` import already covers this usage, to avoid confusion.

**I6. `NotificationsViewModel` line number for `markAsSeen` insertion in `loadNotifications` is off**

The plan says "after line 54, `lastLoadTime = Instant.now()`". In the actual file, `lastLoadTime = Instant.now()` is on line **54**. The `markAsSeen()` call would need to go inside the `.onSuccess` lambda, after line 54. But `markAsSeen()` is a suspend function and it is being called inside a `viewModelScope.launch` block, so it needs to be called with proper coroutine context. Looking at the `.onSuccess` block, it is already inside a `viewModelScope.launch`, so calling `notificationStateManager.markAsSeen()` there is correct. However, `onSuccess` is not a suspending lambda in Kotlin's `Result` type. This is the standard `Result.onSuccess` which takes a non-suspending lambda `(T) -> Unit`. You cannot call `suspend fun markAsSeen()` from inside it.

**This is a compilation error.** The `markAsSeen()` call needs to be moved outside the `.onSuccess` chain, or the approach needs to change. A correct pattern would be:

```kotlin
val result = repository.getNotifications()
result.onSuccess { result ->
    lastLoadTime = Instant.now()
    // ... update state
}
if (result.isSuccess) {
    notificationStateManager.markAsSeen()
}
```

Or use `.also { }` and launch a separate coroutine. The same issue exists for the `refresh()` method in Task 9 Step 4.

---

### Suggestions

**S1. NotificationWorker shows notifications for ALL fetched notifications, not just new ones**

The `showNotifications(notifications)` call passes the entire first page of notifications. This means after the first poll, the user gets system notifications for up to 5 items that may have already been seen. The worker should filter to only notifications newer than the previous `lastPolledId`. This is related to C4 above but specifically about what gets shown vs. what triggers the state update.

**S2. Spec mentions "after login" for permission request, but plan prompts on cold start**

This is a UX concern beyond the technical violation noted in I1. Prompting for notification permissions before the user has even logged in is poor UX and may lead to higher denial rates since the user has no context for why the app needs notifications.

**S3. Missing ProGuard/R8 keep rules step**

The spec notes section mentions verifying ProGuard rules, and Task 12 Step 2 checks for warnings, but there is no explicit step to add keep rules for `NotificationWorker` if needed. Given `isMinifyEnabled = true` in the release build type, and `@HiltWorker` using reflection, a proactive keep rule might be prudent. The existing `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/proguard-rules.pro` has no WorkManager rules.

**S4. The plan replaces entire files (Tasks 4, 7, 11) when targeted edits would be safer**

Replacing `HackersPubApplication.kt`, `AppViewModel.kt`, and `MainActivity.kt` entirely means any changes made to those files between plan authoring and execution will be silently lost. Given that this is a collaborative project (PRs from multiple contributors), targeted edits would be less risky.

**S5. Notification channel ID is duplicated as a string literal**

The channel ID `"hackerspub_notifications"` appears in both `HackersPubApplication` (Task 4) and `NotificationWorker` (Task 6 as `CHANNEL_ID`). A shared constant would be cleaner.

---

### Completeness Check: Plan vs. Spec

| Spec Item | Covered in Plan? | Notes |
|-----------|-----------------|-------|
| WorkManager + Hilt Work deps | Yes (Task 1) | |
| POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission | Yes (Task 2) | |
| Disable default WorkManager init | Yes (Task 2) | |
| NotificationStateManager | Yes (Task 3) | |
| HackersPubApplication as Configuration.Provider | Yes (Task 4) | |
| Notification channel creation | Yes (Task 4) | |
| WorkManager provider in AppModule | Yes (Task 5) | |
| NotificationWorker | Yes (Task 6) | Missing ID comparison (C4) |
| AppViewModel hasUnread + scheduling | Yes (Task 7) | Missing post-login enqueue (I2) |
| HackersPubApp unread dot | Yes (Task 8) | |
| NotificationsViewModel markAsSeen | Yes (Task 9) | Suspend-in-non-suspend lambda issue (I6) |
| Sign-out cleanup | Yes (Task 10) | |
| Permission request timing | Partially (Task 11) | Cold start vs. post-login (I1) |
| "Compare first notification ID against last polled" | **No** | Missing (C4) |
| "Prompt after first successful login, not on cold start" | **No** | Violated (I1) |

### Task Order Assessment

The task order is correct. Dependencies are built before dependents:
1. Dependencies (Task 1) before any code
2. Manifest (Task 2) before Application class changes
3. NotificationStateManager (Task 3) before NotificationWorker (Task 6) and consumers
4. Application (Task 4) and AppModule (Task 5) before Worker (Task 6)
5. Worker (Task 6) before ViewModel wiring (Tasks 7-10)
6. Final verification (Task 12) last

This ordering is sound.

---

### Summary

The plan is structurally solid and covers the vast majority of the spec. The four items requiring attention before implementation are:

1. **C4**: The worker must compare newest notification ID against the previously polled ID to avoid duplicate system notifications on every poll cycle.
2. **I1**: Permission request fires on cold start instead of after login, contradicting the spec.
3. **I2**: Notification polling is not enqueued after a fresh login (only on cold start when already logged in).
4. **I6**: `markAsSeen()` is a suspend function called inside `Result.onSuccess{}` which is not a suspending lambda -- this will not compile.

   144→- [ ] **Step 1: Create the file**
   145→
   146→```kotlin
   147→package pub.hackers.android.data.local
   148→
   149→import androidx.datastore.core.DataStore
   150→import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences
   151→import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.edit
   152→import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.stringPreferencesKey
   153→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
   154→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
   155→import javax.inject.Inject
   156→import javax.inject.Singleton
   157→
   158→@Singleton
   159→class NotificationStateManager @Inject constructor(
   160→    private val dataStore: DataStore<Preferences>
   161→) {
   162→    companion object {
   163→        private val LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID = stringPreferencesKey("last_seen_notification_id")
   164→        private val LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID = stringPreferencesKey("last_polled_notification_id")
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    val hasUnread: Flow<Boolean> = dataStore.data.map { prefs ->
   168→        val lastSeen = prefs[LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID]
   169→        val lastPolled = prefs[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID]
   170→        lastPolled != null && lastPolled != lastSeen
   171→    }
   172→
   173→    suspend fun updateLastPolledId(id: String) {
   174→        dataStore.edit { prefs ->
   175→            prefs[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID] = id
   176→        }
   177→    }
   178→
   179→    suspend fun markAsSeen() {
   180→        dataStore.edit { prefs ->
   181→            val lastPolled = prefs[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID]
   182→            if (lastPolled != null) {
   183→                prefs[LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID] = lastPolled
   184→            }
   185→        }
   186→    }
   187→
   188→    suspend fun clear() {
   189→        dataStore.edit { prefs ->
   190→            prefs.remove(LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID)
   191→            prefs.remove(LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID)
   192→        }
   193→    }
   194→}
   195→```

   233→class HackersPubApplication : Application(), Configuration.Provider {
   234→
   235→    @Inject
   236→    lateinit var workerFactory: HiltWorkerFactory
   237→
   238→    override val workManagerConfiguration: Configuration
   239→        get() = Configuration.Builder()
   240→            .setWorkerFactory(workerFactory)
   241→            .build()

   250→            "hackerspub_notifications",

   253→        ).apply {

   255→        }
   256→        val notificationManager = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
   257→        notificationManager.createNotificationChannel(channel)

   316→### Task 6: Create NotificationWorker
   317→
   318→**Files:**
   319→- Create: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/worker/NotificationWorker.kt`
   320→
   321→- [ ] **Step 1: Create the worker**
   322→
   323→```kotlin
   324→package pub.hackers.android.data.worker
   325→
   326→import android.Manifest
   327→import android.app.NotificationManager
   328→import android.app.PendingIntent
   329→import android.content.Context
   330→import android.content.Intent
   331→import android.content.pm.PackageManager
   332→import android.os.Build
   333→import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
   334→import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
   335→import androidx.hilt.work.HiltWorker
   336→import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
   337→import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
   338→import dagger.assisted.Assisted
   339→import dagger.assisted.AssistedInject
   340→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
   341→import pub.hackers.android.MainActivity
   342→import pub.hackers.android.R
   343→import pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager
   344→import pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager
   345→import pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository
   346→import pub.hackers.android.domain.model.Notification
   347→
   348→@HiltWorker
   349→class NotificationWorker @AssistedInject constructor(
   350→    @Assisted appContext: Context,
   351→    @Assisted workerParams: WorkerParameters,
   352→    private val repository: HackersPubRepository,
   353→    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,
   354→    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager
   355→) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, workerParams) {
   356→
   357→    companion object {
   358→        const val CHANNEL_ID = "hackerspub_notifications"
   359→        const val NOTIFICATION_GROUP = "pub.hackers.android.NOTIFICATIONS"
   360→        const val SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID = 0
   361→    }
   362→
   363→    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
   364→        val isLoggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()
   365→        if (!isLoggedIn) return Result.success()
   366→
   367→        val result = repository.getNotifications(refresh = true)
   368→
   369→        return result.fold(
   370→            onSuccess = { notificationsResult ->
   371→                val notifications = notificationsResult.notifications
   372→                if (notifications.isEmpty()) return@fold Result.success()
   373→
   374→                val newestId = notifications.first().id
   375→                notificationStateManager.updateLastPolledId(newestId)
   376→
   377→                if (hasNotificationPermission()) {
   378→                    showNotifications(notifications)
   379→                }
   380→
   381→                Result.success()
   382→            },
   383→            onFailure = { error ->
   384→                // Auth errors — don't retry
   385→                val message = error.message ?: ""
   386→                if (message.contains("401") || message.contains("403") || message.contains("unauthorized", ignoreCase = true)) {
   387→                    return@fold Result.success()
   388→                }
   389→                // Network errors — retry with exponential backoff
   390→                Result.retry()
   391→            }
   392→        )
   393→    }
   394→
   395→    private fun hasNotificationPermission(): Boolean {
   396→        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
   397→            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
   398→                applicationContext,
   399→                Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS
   400→            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
   401→        } else {
   402→            true
   403→        }
   404→    }
   405→
   406→    private fun showNotifications(notifications: List<Notification>) {
   407→        val notificationManager = applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
   408→
   409→        val intent = Intent(applicationContext, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
   410→            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
   411→            putExtra("navigate_to", "notifications")
   412→        }
   413→        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
   414→            applicationContext,
   415→            0,
   416→            intent,
   417→            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
   418→        )
   419→
   420→        // Show individual notifications (up to 5)
   421→        notifications.take(5).forEachIndexed { index, notification ->
   422→            val text = buildNotificationText(notification)
   423→            val builder = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, CHANNEL_ID)
   424→                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)
   425→                .setContentTitle("Hackers' Pub")
   426→                .setContentText(text)
   427→                .setAutoCancel(true)
   428→                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
   429→                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)
   430→
   431→            notificationManager.notify(index + 1, builder.build())
   432→        }
   433→
   434→        // Summary notification for grouping
   435→        if (notifications.size > 1) {
   436→            val summary = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, CHANNEL_ID)
   437→                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)
   438→                .setContentTitle("Hackers' Pub")
   439→                .setContentText("${notifications.size} new notifications")
   440→                .setAutoCancel(true)
   441→                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
   442→                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)
   443→                .setGroupSummary(true)
   444→                .build()
   445→            notificationManager.notify(SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID, summary)
   446→        }
   447→    }
   448→
   449→    private fun buildNotificationText(notification: Notification): String {
   450→        val actorName = notification.actors.firstOrNull()?.name ?: "Someone"
   451→        return when (notification) {
   452→            is Notification.Follow -> "$actorName followed you"
   453→            is Notification.Mention -> "$actorName mentioned you"
   454→            is Notification.Reply -> "$actorName replied to your post"
   455→            is Notification.Quote -> "$actorName quoted your post"
   456→            is Notification.Share -> "$actorName shared your post"
   457→            is Notification.React -> "$actorName reacted to your post"
   458→        }
   459→    }
   460→}
   461→```
   462→

   466→
   467→Expected: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`
   468→

   483→- [ ] **Step 1: Rewrite AppViewModel**
   484→
   485→Replace the entire file:
   486→
   487→```kotlin
   488→package pub.hackers.android.ui
   489→
   490→import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
   491→import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
   492→import androidx.work.Constraints
   493→import androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy
   494→import androidx.work.NetworkType
   495→import androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder
   496→import androidx.work.WorkManager
   497→import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
   498→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
   499→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
   500→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
   501→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
   502→import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
   503→import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
   504→import pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager
   505→import pub.hackers.android.data.local.PreferencesManager
   506→import pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager
   507→import pub.hackers.android.data.worker.NotificationWorker
   508→import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
   509→import javax.inject.Inject
   510→
   511→@HiltViewModel
   512→class AppViewModel @Inject constructor(
   513→    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,
   514→    val preferencesManager: PreferencesManager,
   515→    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager,
   516→    private val workManager: WorkManager
   517→) : ViewModel() {
   518→
   519→    companion object {
   520→        const val NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME = "notification_poll"
   521→    }
   522→
   523→    val isLoggedIn: Flow<Boolean> = sessionManager.isLoggedIn
   524→
   525→    val hasUnread: StateFlow<Boolean> = notificationStateManager.hasUnread
   526→        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000), false)
   527→
   528→    init {
   529→        ensureNotificationPolling()
   530→    }
   531→
   532→    private fun ensureNotificationPolling() {
   533→        viewModelScope.launch {
   534→            val loggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()
   535→            if (loggedIn) {
   536→                enqueueNotificationPolling()
   537→            }
   538→        }
   539→    }
   540→
   541→    fun enqueueNotificationPolling() {
   542→        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
   543→            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
   544→            .build()
   545→
   546→        val workRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<NotificationWorker>(
   547→            15, TimeUnit.MINUTES
   548→        ).setConstraints(constraints).build()
   549→
   550→        workManager.enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(
   551→            NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME,
   552→            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP,
   553→            workRequest
   554→        )
   555→    }
   556→}
   557→```

   766→**Files:**
   767→- Modify: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/MainActivity.kt:1-60`

   769→- [ ] **Step 1: Add permission request logic**
   770→
   771→Replace the entire file:
   772→
   773→```kotlin
   774→package pub.hackers.android
   775→
   776→import android.Manifest
   777→import android.content.Intent
   778→import android.content.pm.PackageManager
   779→import android.os.Build
   780→import android.os.Bundle
   781→import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
   782→import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
   783→import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
   784→import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
   785→import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
   786→import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
   787→import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
   788→import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
   789→import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
   790→import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
   791→import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
   792→import dagger.hilt.android.AndroidEntryPoint
   793→import pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager
   794→import pub.hackers.android.ui.HackersPubApp
   795→import pub.hackers.android.ui.theme.HackersPubTheme
   796→import pub.hackers.android.ui.theme.LocalAppColors
   797→import javax.inject.Inject
   798→
   799→data class DeepLinkData(
   800→    val token: String,
   801→    val code: String
   802→)
   803→
   804→@AndroidEntryPoint
   805→class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
   806→
   807→    @Inject
   808→    lateinit var sessionManager: SessionManager
   809→
   810→    private var deepLinkData by mutableStateOf<DeepLinkData?>(null)
   811→
   812→    private val requestPermissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
   813→        ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()
   814→    ) { _: Boolean ->
   815→        // Permission result — no action needed, worker checks at post time
   816→    }
   817→
   818→    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
   819→        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
   820→        handleDeepLink(intent)
   821→        requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded()
   822→        enableEdgeToEdge()
   823→        setContent {
   824→            HackersPubTheme {
   825→                Surface(
   826→                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   827→                    color = LocalAppColors.current.background
   828→                ) {
   829→                    HackersPubApp(deepLinkData = deepLinkData)
   830→                }
   831→            }
   832→        }
   833→    }
   834→
   835→    override fun onNewIntent(intent: Intent) {
   836→        super.onNewIntent(intent)
   837→        handleDeepLink(intent)
   838→    }
   839→
   840→    private fun requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded() {
   841→        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
   842→            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
   843→                    this, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS
   844→                ) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
   845→            ) {
   846→                requestPermissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
   847→            }
   848→        }
   849→    }
   850→
   851→    private fun handleDeepLink(intent: Intent?) {
   852→        val data = intent?.data ?: return
   853→        if (data.scheme == "hackerspub" && data.host == "verify") {
   854→            val token = data.getQueryParameter("token")
   855→            val code = data.getQueryParameter("code")
   856→            if (token != null && code != null) {
   857→                deepLinkData = DeepLinkData(token = token, code = code)
   858→            }
   859→        }
   860→    }
   861→}
   862→```

pub.hackers.android.data.local\n\nimport androidx.datastore.core.DataStore\nimport androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences\nimport androidx.datastore.preferences.core.edit\nimport androidx.datastore.preferences.core.stringPreferencesKey\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map\nimport javax.inject.Inject\nimport javax.inject.Singleton\n\n@Singleton\nclass NotificationStateManager @Inject constructor(\n    private val dataStore: DataStore<Preferences>\n) {\n    companion object {\n        private val LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID = stringPreferencesKey(\"last_seen_notification_id\")\n        private val LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID = stringPreferencesKey(\"last_polled_notification_id\")\n    }\n\n    val hasUnread: Flow<Boolean> = dataStore.data.map { prefs ->\n        val lastSeen = prefs[LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID]\n        val lastPolled = prefs[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID]\n        lastPolled != null && lastPolled != lastSeen\n    }\n\n    suspend fun getLastPolledId(): String? {\n        return dataStore.data.map { it[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID] }.first()\n    }\n\n    suspend fun updateLastPolledId(id: String) {\n        dataStore.edit { prefs ->\n            prefs[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID] = id\n        }\n    }\n\n    suspend fun markAsSeen() {\n        dataStore.edit { prefs ->\n            val lastPolled = prefs[LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID]\n            if (lastPolled != null) {\n                prefs[LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID] = lastPolled\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    suspend fun clear() {\n        dataStore.edit { prefs ->\n            prefs.remove(LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID)\n            prefs.remove(LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID)\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify it compiles**\n\nRun: `cd /Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android && ./gradlew avoid duplicate system notifications on every poll cycle. It uses the shared channel ID constant from `HackersPubApplication`.\n\n```kotlin\npackage pub.hackers.android.data.worker\n\nimport android.Manifest\nimport android.app.NotificationManager\nimport android.app.PendingIntent\nimport android.content.Context\nimport android.content.Intent\nimport android.content.pm.PackageManager\nimport android.os.Build\nimport androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat\nimport androidx.core.content.ContextCompat\nimport androidx.hilt.work.HiltWorker\nimport androidx.work.CoroutineWorker\nimport androidx.work.WorkerParameters\nimport dagger.assisted.Assisted\nimport dagger.assisted.AssistedInject\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first\nimport pub.hackers.android.HackersPubApplication\nimport pub.hackers.android.MainActivity\nimport pub.hackers.android.R\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository\nimport pub.hackers.android.domain.model.Notification\n\n@HiltWorker\nclass NotificationWorker @AssistedInject constructor(\n    @Assisted appContext: Context,\n    @Assisted workerParams: WorkerParameters,\n    private val repository: HackersPubRepository,\n    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,\n    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager\n) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, workerParams) {\n\n    companion object {\n        const val NOTIFICATION_GROUP = \"pub.hackers.android.NOTIFICATIONS\"\n        const val SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID = 0\n    }\n\n    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {\n        val isLoggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()\n        if (!isLoggedIn) return Result.success()\n\n        val result = repository.getNotifications(refresh = true)\n\n        return result.fold(\n            onSuccess = { notificationsResult ->\n                val notifications = notificationsResult.notifications\n                if (notifications.isEmpty()) return@fold Result.success()\n\n                val newestId = notifications.first().id\n                val previousId = notificationStateManager.getLastPolledId()\n\n                if (newestId != previousId) {\n                    notificationStateManager.updateLastPolledId(newestId)\n                    if (hasNotificationPermission()) {\n                        showNotifications(notifications)\n                    }\n                }\n\n                Result.success()\n            },\n            onFailure = { error ->\n                // Auth errors — don't retry\n                val message = error.message ?: \"\"\n                if (message.contains(\"401\") || message.contains(\"403\") || message.contains(\"unauthorized\", ignoreCase = true)) {\n                    return@fold Result.success()\n                }\n                // Network errors — retry with exponential backoff\n                Result.retry()\n            }\n        )\n    }\n\n    private fun hasNotificationPermission(): Boolean {\n        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {\n            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(\n                applicationContext,\n                Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS\n            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED\n        } else {\n            true\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun showNotifications(notifications: List<Notification>) {\n        val notificationManager = applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager\n\n        val intent = Intent(applicationContext, MainActivity::class.java).apply {\n            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP\n            putExtra(\"navigate_to\", \"notifications\")\n        }\n        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(\n            applicationContext,\n            0,\n            intent,\n            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE\n        )\n\n        val channelId = HackersPubApplication.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID\n\n        // Show individual notifications (up to 5)\n        notifications.take(5).forEachIndexed { index, notification ->\n            val text = buildNotificationText(notification)\n            val builder = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, channelId)\n                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)\n                .setContentTitle(\"Hackers' Pub\")\n                .setContentText(text)\n                .setAutoCancel(true)\n                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)\n                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)\n\n            notificationManager.notify(index + 1, builder.build())\n        }\n\n        // Summary notification for grouping\n        if (notifications.size > 1) {\n            val summary = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, channelId)\n                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)\n                .setContentTitle(\"Hackers' Pub\")\n                .setContentText(\"${notifications.size} new notifications\")\n                .setAutoCancel(true)\n                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)\n                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)\n                .setGroupSummary(true)\n                .build()\n            notificationManager.notify(SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID, summary)\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun buildNotificationText(notification: Notification): String {\n        val actorName = notification.actors.firstOrNull()?.name ?: \"Someone\"\n        return when (notification) {\n            is Notification.Follow -> \"$actorName followed you\"\n            is Notification.Mention -> \"$actorName mentioned you\"\n            is Notification.Reply -> \"$actorName replied to your post\"\n            is Notification.Quote -> \"$actorName quoted your post\"\n            is Notification.Share -> \"$actorName shared your post\"\n            is Notification.React -> \"$actorName reacted to your post\"\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify it      1→# Implementer Subagent Prompt Template
     2→
     3→Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
     4→
     5→```
     6→Task tool (general-purpose):
     7→  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
     8→  prompt: |
     9→    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
    10→
    11→    ## Task Description
    12→
    13→    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
    14→
    15→    ## Context
    16→
    17→    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
    18→
    19→    ## Before You Begin
    20→
    21→    If you have questions about:
    22→    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
    23→    - The approach or implementation strategy
    24→    - Dependencies or assumptions
    25→    - Anything unclear in the task description
    26→
    27→    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
    28→
    29→    ## Your Job
    30→
    31→    Once you're clear on requirements:
    32→    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
    33→    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
    34→    3. Verify implementation works
    35→    4. Commit your work
    36→    5. Self-review (see below)
    37→    6. Report back
    38→
    39→    Work from: [directory]
    40→
    41→    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
    42→    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
    43→
    44→    ## Code Organization
    45→
    46→    You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more
    47→    reliable when files are focused. Keep this in mind:
    48→    - Follow the file structure defined in the plan
    49→    - Each file should have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface
    50→    - If a file you're creating is growing beyond the plan's intent, stop and report
    51→      it as DONE_WITH_CONCERNS — don't split files on your own without plan guidance
    52→    - If an existing file you're modifying is already large or tangled, work carefully
    53→      and note it as a concern in your report
    54→    - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. Improve code you're touching
    55→      the way a good developer would, but don't restructure things outside your task.
    56→
    57→    ## When You're in Over Your Head
    58→
    59→    It is always OK to stop and say "this is too hard for me." Bad work is worse than
    60→    no work. You will not be penalized for escalating.
    61→
    62→    **STOP and escalate when:**
    63→    - The task requires architectural decisions with multiple valid approaches
    64→    - You need to understand code beyond what was provided and can't find clarity
    65→    - You feel uncertain about whether your approach is correct
    66→    - The task involves restructuring existing code in ways the plan didn't anticipate
    67→    - You've been reading file after file trying to understand the system without progress
    68→
    69→    **How to escalate:** Report back with status BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT. Describe
    70→    specifically what you're stuck on, what you've tried, and what kind of help you need.
    71→    The controller can provide more context, re-dispatch with a more capable model,
    72→    or break the task into smaller pieces.
    73→
    74→    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
    75→
    76→    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
    77→
    78→    **Completeness:**
    79→    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
    80→    - Did I miss any requirements?
    81→    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
    82→
    83→    **Quality:**
    84→    - Is this my best work?
    85→    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
    86→    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
    87→
    88→    **Discipline:**
    89→    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
    90→    - Did I only build what was requested?
    91→    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
    92→
    93→    **Testing:**
    94→    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
    95→    - Did I follow TDD if required?
    96→    - Are tests comprehensive?
    97→
    98→    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
    99→
   100→    ## Report Format
   101→
   102→    When done, report:
   103→    - **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT
   104→    - What you implemented (or what you attempted, if blocked)
   105→    - What you tested and test results
   106→    - Files changed
   107→    - Self-review findings (if any)
   108→    - Any issues or concerns
   109→
   110→    Use DONE_WITH_CONCERNS if you completed the work but have doubts about correctness.
   111→    Use BLOCKED if you cannot complete the task. Use NEEDS_CONTEXT if you need
   112→    information that wasn't provided. Never silently produce work you're unsure about.
   113→```

    14→
    15→    [FULL TEXT of task requirements]
    16→
    17→    ## What Implementer Claims They Built
    18→
    19→    [From implementer's report]
    20→
    21→    ## CRITICAL: Do Not Trust the Report
    22→
    23→    The implementer finished suspiciously quickly. Their report may be incomplete,
    24→    inaccurate, or optimistic. You MUST verify everything independently.
    25→
    26→    **DO NOT:**
    27→    - Take their word for what they implemented
    28→    - Trust their claims about completeness
    29→    - Accept their interpretation of requirements
    30→
    31→    **DO:**
    32→    - Read the actual code they wrote
    33→    - Compare actual implementation to requirements line by line
    34→    - Check for missing pieces they claimed to implement
    35→    - Look for extra features they didn't mention
    36→
    37→    ## Your Job
    38→
    39→    Read the implementation code and verify:
    40→
    41→    **Missing requirements:**
    42→    - Did they implement everything that was requested?
    43→    - Are there requirements they skipped or missed?
    44→    - Did they claim something works but didn't actually implement it?
    45→
    46→    **Extra/unneeded work:**
    47→    - Did they build things that weren't requested?
    48→    - Did they over-engineer or add unnecessary features?
    49→    - Did they add "nice to haves" that weren't in spec?
    50→
    51→    **Misunderstandings:**
    52→    - Did they interpret requirements differently than intended?
    53→    - Did they solve the wrong problem?
    54→    - Did they implement the right feature but wrong way?
    55→
    56→    **Verify by reading code, not by trusting report.**
    57→
    58→    Report:
    59→    - ✅ Spec compliant (if everything matches after code inspection)
    60→    - ❌ Issues found: [list specifically what's missing or extra, with file:line references]
    61→```

{
  "description": "Implement Task 6: Create NotificationWorker",
  "mode": "bypassPermissions",

  "prompt": "You are implementing Task 6: Create NotificationWorker\n\n## Task Description\n\n**File:** Create `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/worker/NotificationWorker.kt`\n\nThis is a `@HiltWorker` extending `CoroutineWorker` that polls for new notifications in the background. Key requirements:\n\n1. Check login state before polling — return success if not logged in\n2. Fetch notifications via repository\n3. **Compare newest notification ID against `getLastPolledId()`** to avoid duplicate system notifications\n4. Only update state and show notifications when the ID differs\n5. Distinguish auth errors (don't retry) from network errors (retry with backoff)\n6. Check POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission before posting\n7. Use shared channel ID constant from `HackersPubApplication.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID`\n8. Group notifications with a summary\n\nCreate the file with this exact content:\n\n```kotlin\npackage pub.hackers.android.data.worker\n\nimport android.Manifest\nimport android.app.NotificationManager\nimport android.app.PendingIntent\nimport android.content.Context\nimport android.content.Intent\nimport android.content.pm.PackageManager\nimport android.os.Build\nimport androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat\nimport androidx.core.content.ContextCompat\nimport androidx.hilt.work.HiltWorker\nimport androidx.work.CoroutineWorker\nimport androidx.work.WorkerParameters\nimport dagger.assisted.Assisted\nimport dagger.assisted.AssistedInject\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first\nimport pub.hackers.android.HackersPubApplication\nimport pub.hackers.android.MainActivity\nimport pub.hackers.android.R\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository\nimport pub.hackers.android.domain.model.Notification\n\n@HiltWorker\nclass NotificationWorker @AssistedInject constructor(\n    @Assisted appContext: Context,\n    @Assisted workerParams: WorkerParameters,\n    private val repository: HackersPubRepository,\n    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,\n    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager\n) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, workerParams) {\n\n    companion object {\n        const val NOTIFICATION_GROUP = \"pub.hackers.android.NOTIFICATIONS\"\n        const val SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID = 0\n    }\n\n    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {\n        val isLoggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()\n        if (!isLoggedIn) return Result.success()\n\n        val result = repository.getNotifications(refresh = true)\n\n        return result.fold(\n            onSuccess = { notificationsResult ->\n                val notifications = notificationsResult.notifications\n                if (notifications.isEmpty()) return@fold Result.success()\n\n                val newestId = notifications.first().id\n                val previousId = notificationStateManager.getLastPolledId()\n\n                if (newestId != previousId) {\n                    notificationStateManager.updateLastPolledId(newestId)\n                    if (hasNotificationPermission()) {\n                        showNotifications(notifications)\n                    }\n                }\n\n                Result.success()\n            },\n            onFailure = { error ->\n                // Auth errors — don't retry\n                val message = error.message ?: \"\"\n                if (message.contains(\"401\") || message.contains(\"403\") || message.contains(\"unauthorized\", ignoreCase = true)) {\n                    return@fold Result.success()\n                }\n                // Network errors — retry with exponential backoff\n                Result.retry()\n            }\n        )\n    }\n\n    private fun hasNotificationPermission(): Boolean {\n        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {\n            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(\n                applicationContext,\n                Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS\n            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED\n        } else {\n            true\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun showNotifications(notifications: List<Notification>) {\n        val notificationManager = applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager\n\n        val intent = Intent(applicationContext, MainActivity::class.java).apply {\n            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP\n            putExtra(\"navigate_to\", \"notifications\")\n        }\n        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(\n            applicationContext,\n            0,\n            intent,\n            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE\n        )\n\n        val channelId = HackersPubApplication.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID\n\n        // Show individual notifications (up to 5)\n        notifications.take(5).forEachIndexed { index, notification ->\n            val text = buildNotificationText(notification)\n            val builder = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, channelId)\n                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)\n                .setContentTitle(\"Hackers' Pub\")\n                .setContentText(text)\n                .setAutoCancel(true)\n                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)\n                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)\n\n            notificationManager.notify(index + 1, builder.build())\n        }\n\n        // Summary notification for grouping\n        if (notifications.size > 1) {\n            val summary = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, channelId)\n                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground)\n                .setContentTitle(\"Hackers' Pub\")\n                .setContentText(\"${notifications.size} new notifications\")\n                .setAutoCancel(true)\n                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)\n                .setGroup(NOTIFICATION_GROUP)\n                .setGroupSummary(true)\n                .build()\n            notificationManager.notify(SUMMARY_NOTIFICATION_ID, summary)\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun buildNotificationText(notification: Notification): String {\n        val actorName = notification.actors.firstOrNull()?.name ?: \"Someone\"\n        return when (notification) {\n            is Notification.Follow -> \"$actorName followed you\"\n            is Notification.Mention -> \"$actorName mentioned you\"\n            is Notification.Reply -> \"$actorName replied to your post\"\n            is Notification.Quote -> \"$actorName quoted your post\"\n            is Notification.Share -> \"$actorName shared your post\"\n            is Notification.React -> \"$actorName reacted to your post\"\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Context\n\n- `HackersPubApplication.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID` was added in the previous task — it's the constant `\"hackerspub_notifications\"`\n- `NotificationStateManager` was created in Task 3 with `getLastPolledId()`, `updateLastPolledId()`, etc.\n- `SessionManager.isLoggedIn` is a `Flow<Boolean>`\n- `HackersPubRepository.getNotifications(refresh: Boolean)` returns `Result<NotificationsResult>` where `NotificationsResult` has `.notifications: List<Notification>`\n- `Notification` is a sealed class with subtypes: Follow, Mention, Reply, Quote, Share, React — each has `actors: List<Actor>` and Actor has `.name`\n\n## Your Job\n\n1. Create the file\n2. Run `./gradlew compileReleaseKotlin` to verify it compiles\n3. Commit: `git commit -m \"feat: add NotificationWorker for background notification polling\"`\n4. Report back\n\nWork from: `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android`"
}

androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy\nimport androidx.work.NetworkType\nimport androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder\nimport androidx.work.WorkManager\nimport dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.PreferencesManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.worker.NotificationWorker\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit\nimport javax.inject.Inject\n\n@HiltViewModel\nclass AppViewModel @Inject constructor(\n    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,\n    val preferencesManager: PreferencesManager,\n    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager,\n    private val workManager: WorkManager\n) : ViewModel() {\n\n    companion object {\n        const val NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME = \"notification_poll\"\n    }\n\n    val isLoggedIn: Flow<Boolean> = sessionManager.isLoggedIn\n\n    val hasUnread: StateFlow<Boolean> = notificationStateManager.hasUnread\n        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000), false)\n\n    init {\n        ensureNotificationPolling()\n    }\n\n    private fun ensureNotificationPolling() {\n        viewModelScope.launch {\n            val loggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()\n            if (loggedIn) {\n                enqueueNotificationPolling()\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fun enqueueNotificationPolling() {\n        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()\n            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)\n            .build()\n\n        val workRequest = NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME,\n            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP,\n            workRequest\n        )\n    }\n}\n```\n\nCommit: `git commit -m \"feat: wire AppViewModel with unread state and ---

# Notification Polling Implementation Review

## Overall Impression

The implementation is faithful to the spec and plan. All 11 tasks from the plan have been completed and committed in the correct order. The code follows existing codebase conventions (Hilt DI patterns, DataStore usage, sealed class models, coroutine-based ViewModels). The architecture -- `NotificationWorker` -> `NotificationStateManager` -> `AppViewModel` -> bottom nav dot -- is clean and well-separated.

---

## 1. Plan Alignment Analysis

### All planned files accounted for

| Planned File | Status |
|---|---|
| `NotificationStateManager.kt` (new) | Implemented, exact match to plan |
| `NotificationWorker.kt` (new) | Implemented, exact match to plan |
| `libs.versions.toml` (modified) | Done |
| `build.gradle.kts` (modified) | Done |
| `AndroidManifest.xml` (modified) | Done |
| `HackersPubApplication.kt` (modified) | Done |
| `AppModule.kt` (modified) | Done |
| `AppViewModel.kt` (modified) | Done |
| `HackersPubApp.kt` (modified) | Done |
| `NotificationsViewModel.kt` (modified) | Done |
| `SettingsViewModel.kt` (modified) | Done |
| `MainActivity.kt` (modified) | Done |

All 11 commits map 1:1 to the 11 plan tasks. No deviations from the planned approach.

### Spec requirements checklist

- [x] WorkManager `PeriodicWorkRequest` with 15-minute interval
- [x] `NetworkType.CONNECTED` constraint
- [x] `ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP` for idempotent enqueue
- [x] `NotificationStateManager` as `@Singleton` with DataStore
- [x] Two separate IDs (`LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID`, `LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID`)
- [x] `hasUnread: Flow<Boolean>` derived from DataStore
- [x] `updateLastPolledId`, `markAsSeen`, `clear` methods
- [x] `@HiltWorker` + `CoroutineWorker` + `@AssistedInject`
- [x] Login check before polling
- [x] Auth error vs network error distinction
- [x] Permission check before posting system notifications
- [x] Notification channel created in `HackersPubApplication.onCreate()`
- [x] Channel ID = `"hackerspub_notifications"`
- [x] Grouped notifications with summary
- [x] `PendingIntent` targeting `MainActivity` with `navigate_to` extra
- [x] `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` permission in manifest
- [x] Default `WorkManagerInitializer` disabled
- [x] `Configuration.Provider` in `HackersPubApplication`
- [x] `AppViewModel` exposes `hasUnread: StateFlow<Boolean>` with `WhileSubscribed(5000)`
- [x] `ensureNotificationPolling()` in `AppViewModel.init`
- [x] Polling enqueued on login transition via `LaunchedEffect(isLoggedIn)`
- [x] `markAsSeen()` on both load and refresh in `NotificationsViewModel`
- [x] Cancel work + clear state + cancel system notifications on sign-out
- [x] `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` permission request gated on login state, Android 13+

---

## 2. Code Quality Assessment

### What was done well

- The `NotificationStateManager` correctly uses a single atomic `edit` block in `markAsSeen()` to read `LAST_POLLED_NOTIFICATION_ID` and write `LAST_SEEN_NOTIFICATION_ID`, avoiding a race condition between read and write.
- The `NotificationWorker.doWork()` properly distinguishes auth errors (return `Result.success()` to avoid futile retries) from transient network errors (return `Result.retry()`).
- The `fold` pattern in the worker is idiomatic Kotlin.
- The `BottomNavItem.hasNotificationDot` field already existed in the codebase with a `false` default -- the implementation correctly wires it up without requiring any component API changes.
- Import ordering and code style are consistent with the rest of the codebase.
- The sign-out flow in `SettingsViewModel` correctly orders operations: cancel work first, then clear notification state, then dismiss system notifications, then clear session and Apollo cache.

---

## 3. Issues Found

### IMPORTANT: `navigate_to` intent extra is not handled

**File:** `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/worker/NotificationWorker.kt` (line 91)
**File:** `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/MainActivity.kt`

The worker creates a `PendingIntent` with `putExtra("navigate_to", "notifications")`, but `MainActivity` never reads this extra. When a user taps a system notification, the app opens but does not navigate to the notifications screen. The `handleDeepLink` method only handles the `hackerspub://verify` URI scheme.

The spec says:
> PendingIntent targets MainActivity with an extra/URI that the Compose nav controller routes to the notifications screen

This is a missing piece. To fix it, `MainActivity.onCreate()` (and `onNewIntent()`) would need to check for `intent?.getStringExtra("navigate_to")` and pass it through to the Compose layer, similar to how `deepLinkData` is handled.

**Severity:** Important -- system notifications will open the app but won't navigate to the notification screen, which is a degraded user experience.

### IMPORTANT: No ProGuard keep rule for `NotificationWorker`

**File:** `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/proguard-rules.pro`

The spec explicitly notes:
> Verify that hilt-work's bundled consumer rules are sufficient for @HiltWorker with isMinifyEnabled = true. Add explicit keep rules for NotificationWorker if needed.

The build has `isMinifyEnabled = true`, but no keep rule was added for `NotificationWorker`. While `hilt-work` 1.2.0 ships consumer ProGuard rules that should keep `@HiltWorker`-annotated classes, this is a known fragile area. The plan's Task 12 (verification step) calls for checking for R8 issues, but there is no evidence this was verified, and no keep rule was added as a safety measure.

**Severity:** Important -- may cause a crash in release builds if R8 strips the worker class. A one-line addition to `proguard-rules.pro` would eliminate the risk:
```
-keep class pub.hackers.android.data.worker.NotificationWorker { *; }
```

### Suggestion: `runBlocking` in `MainActivity.requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded()`

**File:** `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/MainActivity.kt` (line 71)

The method uses `runBlocking { sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first() }` on the main thread. This reads from DataStore, which involves file I/O on first access. While DataStore typically caches after the first read and the plan explicitly specifies this approach, it is worth noting that this blocks the main thread during `onCreate()`. In practice the DataStore file is small and reads complete in under a millisecond, so this is acceptable for now, but a `lifecycleScope.launch` approach would be slightly better.

**Severity:** Suggestion -- low risk in practice, but worth considering for future improvement.

### Suggestion: Notification IDs could collide across poll cycles

**File:** `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/worker/NotificationWorker.kt` (lines 103, 113)

The worker uses `index + 1` (values 1 through 5) as notification IDs. On the next poll cycle, if there are new notifications, they will replace the previous ones since the same IDs are reused. This is actually the desired behavior for a polling-based system (you don't want stale notifications to accumulate), so this is fine. Just noting it for awareness.

**Severity:** None -- by design.

### Suggestion: `showNotifications` shows all fetched notifications, not just the new ones

**File:** `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/worker/NotificationWorker.kt` (lines 86-128)

When `newestId != previousId`, the worker calls `showNotifications(notifications)` with the full list of fetched notifications (up to 5). This means if there is 1 new notification and 4 old ones, all 5 will appear as system notifications. The spec says "Build and post Android system notifications for the new items," implying only truly new items should be shown. However, determining which items are "new" would require comparing each notification ID against the previous polled ID, which adds complexity. Given the 15-minute poll interval and the fact that notifications are capped at 5 and auto-cancel, this is a minor UX issue.

**Severity:** Suggestion -- for a v1 implementation this is acceptable, but a future improvement could filter to only notifications newer than `previousId`.

---

## 4. Architecture and Design

The implementation correctly follows the architecture laid out in the spec:

- **Separation of concerns:** `NotificationStateManager` owns the DataStore keys and exposes a clean API. The worker only writes; the UI only reads.
- **Loose coupling:** `SettingsViewModel` references `AppViewModel.NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME` as a constant, which is a minor coupling but acceptable since it's just a string constant.
- **Lifecycle correctness:** `StateFlow` with `WhileSubscribed(5000)` in `AppViewModel` is the standard pattern for this codebase. The `stateIn` call ensures the flow is only active when the UI is observing.
- **Hilt integration:** The `@HiltWorker` + `Configuration.Provider` pattern is correctly implemented. The `SessionManager` explicit `@Provides` in `AppModule` is consistent with how the codebase already provides it (rather than using `@Inject` constructor binding alone).

---

## 5. Summary

| Category | Count |
|---|---|
| Spec requirements met | 22/23 |
| Critical issues | 0 |
| Important issues | 2 |
| Suggestions | 2 |

**Important issues:**
1. The `navigate_to` intent extra in `NotificationWorker` is never consumed by `MainActivity`, so tapping a system notification opens the app but does not navigate to the notifications screen.
2. No ProGuard keep rule for `NotificationWorker` despite `isMinifyEnabled = true` in the release build type.

---

## Final Assessment: **ISSUES_FOUND**

The implementation is thorough and closely follows both the spec and plan. Code quality is high and patterns are consistent with the existing codebase. However, the two Important issues should be addressed before merging: (1) the `navigate_to` intent extra is dead code -- system notification taps will not route to the notifications screen, and (2) a ProGuard keep rule should be added to protect the worker class in release builds. Both are straightforward fixes.

> AGENT

All implementation done. Here's the summary:

**12 commits** implementing background notification polling via WorkManager:

1. WorkManager + Hilt Work dependencies
2. `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` permission + disable default initializer
3. `NotificationStateManager` — tracks unread state via DataStore
4. `HackersPubApplication` — HiltWorkerFactory + notification channel
5. `AppModule` — provides WorkManager
6. `NotificationWorker` — polls every 15 min, compares IDs, posts system notifications
7. `AppViewModel` — exposes `hasUnread`, schedules polling
8. `HackersPubApp` — wires unread dot, enqueues polling on login
9. `NotificationsViewModel` — marks as seen on load/refresh
10. `SettingsViewModel` — cancels work + clears notifications on sign-out
11. `MainActivity` — requests notification permission (gated on login)
12. Review fixes — handles `navigate_to` intent + ProGuard keep rule

To answer your question about the **polling interval**: it's set to **15 minutes**, which is the minimum Android allows for `PeriodicWorkRequest`. It only runs when the device has network connectivity.

Want me to push this and create a PR?

> DEVELOPER

How is the polling interval?

> AGENT

**15 minutes** — the minimum Android allows for `PeriodicWorkRequest`. It's set in `AppViewModel.enqueueNotificationPolling()`:

```kotlin
PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<NotificationWorker>(
    15, TimeUnit.MINUTES
).setConstraints(constraints).build()
```

A few notes:
- Android may defer execution slightly beyond 15 min based on battery optimization (Doze mode, app standby)
- It only runs when the device has network connectivity (`NetworkType.CONNECTED` constraint)
- It's the best we can do without backend WebPush support — anything shorter isn't possible with WorkManager

> DEVELOPER

Can we parameterize? 
For example, not connected 15 minutes, but connected 1minutes

> AGENT

WorkManager enforces a **minimum 15-minute interval** for `PeriodicWorkRequest` — Android doesn't allow shorter background polling to preserve battery.

However, we can use a **dual strategy**:

| State | Mechanism | Interval |
|-------|-----------|----------|
| **App in foreground** | Coroutine timer in `AppViewModel` | Configurable (e.g. 1 min) |
| **App in background** | WorkManager | 15 min (Android minimum) |

The foreground polling would use a simple `while(true) { delay(interval); poll() }` loop in `viewModelScope`, which automatically cancels when the app goes to background. WorkManager continues handling the background case.

Want me to implement this dual approach? The user could configure the foreground interval in settings.

> DEVELOPER

Yes, go

> AGENT

Let me implement the dual-strategy polling. The changes are focused — mainly `AppViewModel` for foreground polling.
