```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/AppViewModel.kt",
  "content": "package pub.hackers.android.ui\n\nimport androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel\nimport androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope\nimport androidx.work.Constraints\nimport androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy\nimport androidx.work.NetworkType\nimport androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder\nimport androidx.work.WorkManager\nimport dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.Job\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.delay\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.isActive\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.launch\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.NotificationStateManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.PreferencesManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.local.SessionManager\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.worker.NotificationWorker\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit\nimport javax.inject.Inject\n\n@HiltViewModel\nclass AppViewModel @Inject constructor(\n    private val sessionManager: SessionManager,\n    val preferencesManager: PreferencesManager,\n    private val notificationStateManager: NotificationStateManager,\n    private val workManager: WorkManager,\n    private val repository: HackersPubRepository\n) : ViewModel() {\n\n    companion object {\n        const val NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME = \"notification_poll\"\n        const val FOREGROUND_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 60_000L // 1 minute\n    }\n\n    val isLoggedIn: Flow<Boolean> = sessionManager.isLoggedIn\n\n    val hasUnread: StateFlow<Boolean> = notificationStateManager.hasUnread\n        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000), false)\n\n    private var foregroundPollJob: Job? = null\n\n    init {\n        ensureNotificationPolling()\n    }\n\n    private fun ensureNotificationPolling() {\n        viewModelScope.launch {\n            val loggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()\n            if (loggedIn) {\n                enqueueNotificationPolling()\n                startForegroundPolling()\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fun enqueueNotificationPolling() {\n        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()\n            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)\n            .build()\n\n        val workRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<NotificationWorker>(\n            15, TimeUnit.MINUTES\n        ).setConstraints(constraints).build()\n\n        workManager.enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(\n            NOTIFICATION_WORK_NAME,\n            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP,\n            workRequest\n        )\n    }\n\n    fun startForegroundPolling() {\n        if (foregroundPollJob?.isActive == true) return\n        foregroundPollJob = viewModelScope.launch {\n            while (isActive) {\n                delay(FOREGROUND_POLL_INTERVAL_MS)\n                pollNotifications()\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fun stopForegroundPolling() {\n        foregroundPollJob?.cancel()\n        foregroundPollJob = null\n    }\n\n    private suspend fun pollNotifications() {\n        val loggedIn = sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()\n        if (!loggedIn) return\n\n        repository.getNotifications(refresh = true)\n            .onSuccess { result ->\n                val notifications = result.notifications\n                if (notifications.isNotEmpty()) {\n                    val newestId = notifications.first().id\n                    val previousId = notificationStateManager.getLastPolledId()\n                    if (newestId != previousId) {\n                        notificationStateManager.updateLastPolledId(newestId)\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n    }\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's how the dual strategy works:

| State | Mechanism | Interval | Shows system notifications? |
|-------|-----------|----------|-----------------------------|
| **App visible** | Coroutine in `viewModelScope` | **1 minute** | No (user is in the app, just updates the dot) |
| **App in background** | WorkManager | **15 minutes** (Android minimum) | Yes |

The foreground poller automatically starts on `ON_START` and stops on `ON_STOP`, so it has zero battery impact when the app is backgrounded. The `FOREGROUND_POLL_INTERVAL_MS` constant in `AppViewModel` can easily be made configurable via settings later.

```
   123→    suspend fun getNotifications(after: String? = null, refresh: Boolean = false): Result<NotificationsResult> {
   124→        return try {
   125→            val response = apolloClient.query(NotificationsQuery(Optional.presentIfNotNull(after)))
   126→                .apply { if (refresh) fetchPolicy(FetchPolicy.NetworkOnly) }
   127→                .execute()
   128→
   129→            if (response.hasErrors()) {
   130→                Result.failure(Exception(response.errors?.firstOrNull()?.message ?: "Unknown error"))
   131→            } else {
   132→                val data = response.data?.viewer?.notifications
   133→                Result.success(
   134→                    NotificationsResult(
   135→                        notifications = data?.edges?.mapNotNull { edge ->
   136→                            edge.node.toNotification()
   137→                        } ?: emptyList(),
   138→                        hasNextPage = data?.pageInfo?.hasNextPage ?: false,
   139→                        endCursor = data?.pageInfo?.endCursor
   140→                    )
   141→                )
   142→            }
   143→        } catch (e: Exception) {
   144→            Result.failure(e)
   145→        }
   146→    }
```

```
    96→        ) {
    97→            when {
    98→                uiState.isLoading && uiState.notifications.isEmpty() -> {
    99→                    FullScreenLoading()
   100→                }
   101→                uiState.error != null && uiState.notifications.isEmpty() -> {
   102→                    ErrorMessage(
   103→                        message = uiState.error ?: stringResource(R.string.error_generic),
   104→                        onRetry = { viewModel.refresh() }
   105→                    )
   106→                }
   107→                uiState.notifications.isEmpty() -> {
   108→                    Box(
   109→                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   110→                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   111→                    ) {
   112→                        Text(
   113→                            text = stringResource(R.string.no_notifications),
   114→                            style = typography.bodyLarge,
   115→                            color = colors.textSecondary,
   116→                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
   117→                        )
   118→                    }
   119→                }
   120→                else -> {
   121→                    PullToRefreshBox(
   122→                        isRefreshing = uiState.isRefreshing,
   123→                        onRefresh = { viewModel.refresh() }
   124→                    ) {
   125→                        LazyColumn(state = listState) {
   126→                            items(
   127→                                items = uiState.notifications,
   128→                                key = { it.id }
   129→                            ) { notification ->
   130→                                NotificationItem(
   131→                                    notification = notification,
   132→                                    onPostClick = onPostClick,
   133→                                    onProfileClick = onProfileClick
   134→                                )
   135→                                HorizontalDivider(
   136→                                    color = colors.divider,
   137→                                    thickness = 1.dp,
   138→                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
   139→                                )
   140→                            }
   141→
   142→                            if (uiState.isLoadingMore) {
   143→                                item {
   144→                                    LoadingItem()
   145→                                }
   146→                            }
   147→                        }
   148→                    }
   149→                }
   150→            }
   151→        }
   152→    }
   153→}
   154→
   155→@Composable
   156→private fun NotificationItem(
   157→    notification: Notification,
   158→    onPostClick: (String) -> Unit,
   159→    onProfileClick: (String) -> Unit
   160→) {
   161→    val colors = LocalAppColors.current
   162→    val typography = LocalAppTypography.current
   163→
   164→    val (icon, actionText) = when (notification) {
   165→        is Notification.Follow -> Pair(
   166→            Icons.Default.PersonAdd,
   167→            stringResource(R.string.notification_follow)
   168→        )
   169→        is Notification.Mention -> Pair(
   170→            Icons.Outlined.AlternateEmail,
   171→            stringResource(R.string.notification_mention)
   172→        )
   173→        is Notification.Reply -> Pair(
   174→            Icons.Default.Reply,
   175→            stringResource(R.string.notification_reply)
   176→        )
   177→        is Notification.Quote -> Pair(
   178→            Icons.Default.FormatQuote,
   179→            stringResource(R.string.notification_quote)
   180→        )
   181→        is Notification.Share -> Pair(
   182→            Icons.Default.Repeat,
   183→            stringResource(R.string.notification_share)
   184→        )
   185→        is Notification.React -> Pair(
   186→            Icons.Default.Favorite,
   187→            notification.emoji?.let { "$it" } ?: stringResource(R.string.notification_react)
   188→        )
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    val post = when (notification) {
   192→        is Notification.Mention -> notification.post
   193→        is Notification.Reply -> notification.post
   194→        is Notification.Quote -> notification.post
   195→        is Notification.Share -> notification.post
   196→        is Notification.React -> notification.post
   197→        else -> null
   198→    }
   199→
   200→    val actor = notification.actors.firstOrNull()
   201→
   202→    Row(
   203→        modifier = Modifier
   204→            .fillMaxWidth()
   205→            .clickable {
   206→                when {
   207→                    post != null -> onPostClick(post.id)
   208→                    actor != null -> onProfileClick(actor.handle)
   209→                }
   210→            }
   211→            .padding(12.dp),
   212→        verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
   213→    ) {
   214→        Icon(
   215→            imageVector = icon,
   216→            contentDescription = null,
   217→            tint = colors.textSecondary,
   218→            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
   219→        )
   220→
   221→        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
   222→
   223→        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
   224→            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
   225→                if (actor != null) {
   226→                    AsyncImage(
   227→                        model = actor.avatarUrl,
   228→                        contentDescription = null,
   229→                        modifier = Modifier
   230→                            .size(AppShapes.avatarNotification)
   231→                            .clip(CircleShape)
   232→                            .clickable { onProfileClick(actor.handle) },
   233→                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
   234→                    )
   235→                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
   236→                }
   237→
   238→                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
   239→                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
   240→                        Text(
   241→                            text = actor?.name ?: actor?.handle ?: "Someone",
   242→                            style = typography.bodyLargeSemiBold,
   243→                            color = colors.textPrimary,
   244→                            maxLines = 1,
   245→                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
   246→                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
   247→                        )
   248→                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
   249→                        Text(
   250→                            text = actionText,
   251→                            style = typography.bodyLarge,
   252→                            color = colors.textBody
   253→                        )
   254→                    }
   255→                    Text(
   256→                        text = formatRelativeTime(notification.created),
   257→                        style = typography.labelMedium,
   258→                        color = colors.textSecondary
   259→                    )
   260→                }
   261→            }
   262→
   263→            if (post != null) {
   264→                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.size(8.dp))
   265→                HtmlContent(
   266→                    html = post.content,
   267→                    maxLines = 3,
   268→                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
   269→                )
   270→            }
   271→        }
   272→    }
   273→}
   274→
   275→private fun formatRelativeTime(instant: Instant): String {
   276→    val now = Instant.now()
   277→    val duration = Duration.between(instant, now)
   278→
   279→    return when {
   280→        duration.toMinutes() < 1 -> "now"
   281→        duration.toMinutes() < 60 -> "${duration.toMinutes()}m"
   282→        duration.toHours() < 24 -> "${duration.toHours()}h"
   283→        duration.toDays() < 7 -> "${duration.toDays()}d"
   284→        else -> "${duration.toDays() / 7}w"
   285→    }
   286→}
   287→
```

Users receive Android system notifications for new items and see an unread dot on the bottom nav tab.\n\nKey changes:\n- **NotificationStateManager**: Tracks unread state via DataStore (last-seen vs last-polled IDs)\n- **NotificationWorker**: `@HiltWorker` that polls the Notifications GraphQL query, compares IDs, and posts grouped system notifications\n- **Dual polling strategy**: WorkManager for background (15 min, Android minimum), coroutine timer for foreground (1 min, lifecycle-aware)\n- **UI wiring**: Unread dot on bottom nav, mark-as-seen on notifications screen load/refresh\n- **Lifecycle management**: Polling enqueued on login, cancelled on sign-out (with system notification dismissal)\n- **Simplified Notifications query**: Replaced heavy `PostFields` fragment with just `id` + `content` (the only fields the notifications screen uses)\n- **POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission**: Requested on Android 13+, gated on login state\n\nCloses #67\n\n## Test plan\n- [ ] Verify unread notification dot appears on bottom nav when new notifications arrive\n- [ ] Verify dot clears when opening the notifications screen\n- [ ] Verify system notifications appear in background after ~15 minutes\n- [ ] Verify tapping a system notification opens the app to notifications screen\n- [ ] Verify sign-out cancels polling and dismisses system notifications\n- [ ] Verify POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission dialog appears on Android 13+ after login\n- [ ] Verify release APK builds without ProGuard issues\nEOF\n)\"",

> AGENT

PR created: https://github.com/hackers-pub/android/pull/71

> DEVELOPER

15 MINUTE is the best? really?

> AGENT

Yes, unfortunately. Android's `PeriodicWorkRequest` enforces a **hard minimum of 15 minutes** — this is baked into the OS, not a library choice. Calling it with anything less silently clamps to 15.

That said, in practice the actual interval is often **longer** than 15 minutes due to:
- **Doze mode** — delays background work when the device is idle
- **App Standby Buckets** — if the user rarely opens the app, Android throttles further (up to hours)
- **Battery Saver** — can defer all background work

This is exactly why we added the **foreground coroutine poller at 1 minute** — when the user actually has the app open, they get near-real-time updates. The 15-minute WorkManager poll is just a fallback for when the app is closed.

The only way to get faster background notifications would be a push-based approach (FCM/UnifiedPush), which requires backend WebPush support that Hackers' Pub doesn't have yet.

> DEVELOPER

How notification spawned? What criteria?

> AGENT

The worker shows system notifications when **all three** conditions are met:

1. **User is logged in** — `sessionManager.isLoggedIn.first()` is true
2. **Newest notification ID differs from last polled ID** — meaning there's something new since last check
3. **POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission is granted** — Android 13+ requires explicit permission

The problem is: when condition 2 triggers, it shows **up to 5 notifications from the fetched page**, not just the truly new ones. So if 1 new notification arrived and 4 old ones are in the list, all 5 get shown as system notifications.

Want me to fix this so it only shows notifications that are actually newer than the previous poll?

> DEVELOPER

When get notified, do we have also indicator for unread? on bottom nav bar?

> AGENT

Yes — the unread dot on the bottom nav is already wired up. Here's how it flows:

1. **Worker/foreground poller** calls `notificationStateManager.updateLastPolledId(newestId)` when new notifications are detected
2. **`NotificationStateManager.hasUnread`** emits `true` when `lastPolledId != lastSeenId`
3. **`AppViewModel.hasUnread`** collects that flow as a `StateFlow`
4. **`HackersPubApp`** passes `hasNotificationDot = hasUnread` to the bottom nav item

The dot clears when the user opens the notifications screen — `NotificationsViewModel` calls `markAsSeen()` on every successful load/refresh, which sets `lastSeenId = lastPolledId`.

So both the system notification (background) and the unread dot (in-app) work from the same state.
